the typed fallbacks and the screen-reader names in `data/visit/location.php`; the tables in `visit-page-copy-en.md` / `visit-page-copy-ru-draft.md`. **Database step for the test and main sites:** `WP_ROOT=<site root> php tools/field-update.php tools/field-updates/2026-09-27-visit-landmarks.json` (safe twice; skips any field edited since). **Desktop:** the name wraps there too now (the `nowrap` + ellipsis dropped) — «Ярославская Стрелка» would have been the third name cut in the 172px cell after «Богоявленская площадь»; measured at 1440 / 1200 / 992, the two wrapped Russian badges make the column 425px against the map's 440, the English column is unchanged; the brief's two open items on the abbreviations and the ellipsis are settled. **Tool fix on the way (`tools/field-update.php`):** a group's sub-field written by its key is saved by SCF under the bare sub-field name (`name_ru`), so the first run reported "applied" twice and changed nothing; a saved field is now written by its name (the reference resolves it), the key only for a field never saved on the page; the stray `name_ru` / `name_en` meta on the Visit page was removed. ## Dream Team wall strip — a mouse can move it, and it shows that it moves — 27 September 2026

Author: the strip scrolls with a touchpad but not with a mouse. **Cause:** `.about-team__drift` is an `overflow-x: auto` box with its scrollbar hidden and no script — a touchpad sends horizontal deltas, a mouse wheel vertical ones only, and browsers never turn those sideways; with no bar to drag, a mouse had no handle at all (Shift + wheel worked, undiscoverable). Two ideas weighed: a scroll-linked drift (the strip moving as the section scrolls — a 2,800px strip against one viewport of travel means a sticky pin and a new About rhythm, and two inputs on one position) and an entrance roll (the menu rail's pattern — a hint, not a handle). **Built, `js/about-drift.js`:** (1) *wheel → sideways* — a vertical wheel over the strip scrolls it while it can still move that way; at either end the event passes and the page scrolls on, so the strip never traps the reader; horizontal deltas pass untouched; mid-sequence events the browser has latched (`!cancelable`) pass. The trade, on record: a two-finger vertical swipe over the strip moves the strip until it reaches an end. (2) *one drift on entrance* — when the strip crosses into the top two thirds of the screen it drifts 96px (a third of a card) and settles back over 1.4s on a sine, once; off under `prefers-reduced-motion`, in a hidden tab, or once the guest has moved it; stopped by the first wheel, touch, pointer or key on the strip (the rail's "stops for good"). Verified in headless Chrome at 1440 on `/about/` with real wheel events: the entrance hump (0 → 93 → 0), three notches over the strip move it and not the page, one notch at the end moves the page, a horizontal delta scrolls natively, and nothing moves under reduced motion. Not built: drag-to-scroll with the mouse (the other honest option; the cards are not links). *To judge on the author's mouse and touchpad.*

## TEMPORARY — Google Maps for every guest, to test from Russia — 27 September 2026

*Resolved the same evening: the team confirmed it works, Google is the rule now — the entry above.*

The author wants to see whether the Google My Maps embed works on the Russian site. `location-map.js` picks the provider by the visitor's timezone / browser language, not by the site language (Russian zones and `ru-*` → Yandex, everyone else → Google; the home Contacts map is a hard-coded Google iframe for everyone). A `TEST_PROVIDER = 'google'` constant now short-circuits the detection, so the About, Visit and Menu maps load Google for every guest; `?map=yandex` / `?map=google` on any page with a map overrides both, for comparing the two on one device. **To revert:** set `TEST_PROVIDER` back to `null` and rebuild — the detection is untouched. Verified on the private server with a Moscow timezone: `/visit/` and `/about/` → google.com, `/visit/?map=yandex` → yandex.ru, `/en/visit/` (Paris) → google.com.
## Form fields in their Figma states; the autofill box gone — 27 September 2026

The author's two notes from the live site (iPad Safari, iPhone Chrome, 26 Sep): the field icon never left Mushroom, and an autofilled contact sat in a black (Safari) or light-blue (Chrome for iOS) box. **Field states** (Figma `field-singleLine` 265:3459 night / 466:19366 day): the leading icon takes the accent — Avocado by day, Lime by night and on the Visit form — while the field is hovered (pointer devices only) or focused, and keeps it once the value passes (`state=filled-passed`); a passing value shows the 12px checkmark at the trailing edge (`c-checkmark.svg`), the error state the «!» (`exclamation.svg`, recoloured from its Lemon fill) with the icon in Paprika. Both marks ship in the markup of every single-line field (contact form × 3, team form × 2), decorative; contacts.css shows them from `.is-valid` / `.contact-field--error`. **Live validation** is new: `js/field-state.js` watches every `.contact-field` on `input` / `change`, toggles `.is-valid` and clears an error the moment the value passes; the same validators judge the submit (`isValidEmail`, `isValidPhone` — six digits or more, the shortest number the bar could call back — replacing the old "not empty" phone check). The email ↔ phone swap and `form.reset()` refresh the state. **Autofill:** the old override painted a fixed Peppercorn inset shadow, which is the desktop card's ground but not the phone form's Soft Peppercorn — hence the black box. The wrap now carries `--field-bg` / `--field-text` (Parchment · Peppercorn by day; Peppercorn · Paper by night and on Visit; Soft Peppercorn on the Visit phone form) and one rule in contacts.css covers the browser's tint with them (`:-webkit-autofill` and `:autofill` as separate rules); Chrome for iOS sets its blue inline on the element, so the input's own `background` is `transparent !important`. Verified on a private PHP server with the worktree theme (Visit phone 393 / desktop 1440, home day / night, the About team dialog): icon and border colours per state, the marks' display, `--field-bg` per context, an autofill simulation with the built declarations leaving no box. **Not built:** the `focused-typing` state's clear «×» (Figma) and any mark on the multi-line field (its trailing slot is hidden in the form frame). **Noted, not touched:** the Figma component's focused border is Avocado in both files where the build uses Lime; the team dialog under the night theme inherits the night field rules on a Paper card (Paper labels and text on Paper). Docs: this entry, the file table, *Autofill Override* below. **Later the same day:** the fix vanished from sweet-pepper-bar.local — the cookie-notice session had rebuilt `dist` from its own worktree and synced it into the main checkout, whose `src` still held the rules; rebuilt in the main checkout from its sources (both sessions' work). **Success state on phones (author):** the two info boxes are Soft Peppercorn on the Soft Peppercorn form, so their 16px inset only held the blocks 56px apart — `padding: 0` in the phone block, 24 between them (the content gap); restored where the ground differs. **Form card from 600px (author's iPad mini, 744):** the full-bleed phone form read too wide on the device — a `600–767` block gives the form the desktop card back (Mushroom hairline, 8px radius, 32 inset, Peppercorn ground, the card's shadow, `--field-bg` Peppercorn, the info boxes' inset back) inside the phone composition; the booking block and the topic chips stay. 600 is a working threshold — the widest phones are ~440, the smallest tablets 744. Measured at 393 / 744 / 768: form 393 full-bleed, 712 card, 736 card; box gap 24 / 24 / 24, item → next label 24 / 56 / 56.

## SEO & accessibility pass — alt text, icons, headings, search and sharing — 25 September 2026

Author: "review each page and asset in WordPress and improve them for SEO and accessibility, especially the alt text for icons and images." Audited the ten rendered pages (5 × RU / EN) and all 63 Media Library items.

**Found:** no image in the Media Library had alt text; the Russian pages read English alts (home hero tiles, previews, guests, team, stamp) and several English alts described the wrong photo ("Live DJ set" on a copper-mug cocktail, "Cocktail", "Community event"); ~60–90 inline SVG icons per page exposed to screen readers; every connector word read twice (the reflection carried the same alt); menu pages had no `<h1>`, About had two; About and Visit had no description and their tabs read "About" / "Visit" on the Russian site; no Open Graph tags, no structured data; the theme had no `.screen-reader-text` rule of its own.

**Theme:**
- `inc/inline-svg.php` — `sweet_pepper_inline_svg( $path, $label = '' )`: decorative by default (`aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"`); a label makes it `role="img"`. The four `file_get_contents()` icon inlines now go through it; Phosphor `<i>` icons carry `aria-hidden`.
- Dish icons are announced by meaning (`dish-row.php`): Вегетарианское · Хит · Острое · Ярославское блюдо — the legend in `design.md` → Dish icons.
- `section-link-word.php` — a `section-link-word--reflection` word has `alt=""` and `aria-hidden` (read once, not twice).
- Menu pages: a screen-reader `<h1>` from the page's «Поиск» title (`menu-hero.php`); the hero connector's alt is translated. About's closing headline `h1` → `h2`.
- Social icons in header and footer: the drawer's pattern — a translated `aria-label` on the link, `alt=""` on the icon. The "See all events" card photo is `alt=""` (the link's words say it). Footer stamp alt translatable.
- `.screen-reader-text` + a visible-on-focus skip link in `main.css`.
- **`inc/seo.php`** (new): «Поиск» for About and Visit (`about_seo_*` / `visit_seo_*` tabs, generator `tools/page-field-groups.py`; drafts in `data/about/seo.php`, `data/visit/seo.php`, marked *draft*); one meta-description printer for every page (the home / menu printers retired); Open Graph + Twitter tags with a per-page photo; `BarOrPub` + `Restaurant` JSON-LD on home and Visit — address, hours from Bar Settings, both menus, VK / Instagram. **Phone and e-mail left out** — not yet confirmed for publishing (`inc/visit-data.php`).
- VK cards: `news_alt` empty → the photo's Media Library alt → the caption (`inc/vk-feed.php`); the field's admin placeholder now says so («пусто — из медиатеки, иначе как подпись», `group_sp_news.json`).
- Also: the event card's hand-written arrow SVG is `aria-hidden`; the menu hero's **phone** connector (FOOD MENU / DRINKS MENU, already classed as a reflection) is now silent too — the page's `<h1>` names the page.
- Docs: `website-brief.md` (Platform → SEO line; About row 12 and Visit's seventh tab «Поиск»), `design.md` → Dish icons (announced names).
- **Merging:** `dist/` was rebuilt here; the main checkout's other session rebuilt it too — run `npm run build` after the merge rather than resolving hashed files by hand.
- `languages/ru_RU.l10n.php`: the alt strings joined (they were deferred).

**WordPress content (local DB, backup taken first):** Russian alt text on all 63 Media Library items, written from looking at each photo; RU twins filled on «Главная» (hero ×4, previews, about, event cards) and About (founder, guests ×8); inaccurate EN alts corrected. Typed fallbacks in `data/home/*.php`, `data/about/guests.php`, `data/about/story.php` match.

**Verified** on a private server with the worktree theme (RU + EN, all pages): one `<h1>` each, zero exposed decorative SVGs, every visible image with a correct-language alt or deliberately silent; menu hero pixel-identical; skip link shows on Tab.

**Open (author's call):** the home `<h1>` is the daypart greeting ("ВКУСНОЕ УТРО!") — fine for people, weak for search; menu highlight cards use placeholder photos (Фетучини Корфу shows the жаркое), so their dish-name alts don't match the picture yet; the team and contact forms' English headings on RU pages stay (deferred by the drafts); `group_sp_menu_food/bar.json` are out of step with the generator (untouched here); the SEO drafts want the author's wording.

## Vacancies — a posting with its own page, one control for post / close / republish — 25 September 2026

The team wants to post openings on the website too — to share with their network, hh.ru kept as an option (author, after the team's update). Proposal → agreed → built the same day; plan and rules: `website-brief.md` → Content editing → *Vacancies*.
- **Records:** `vacancy` («Вакансии», `inc/cpt.php`) — the one public post type: `/vacancies/<slug>/` + `/en/…` (`SWEET_PEPPER_REWRITE_VERSION` 3 — the rules were flushed into the shared local DB from the worktree run). The block editor is off for the type as for pages (`functions.php`), so the field form is the edit screen. Fields `acf-json/group_sp_vacancy.json` (generator): title EN, department, **«На сайте»** (В архиве · 1 · 2 · 4 недели), hh.ru, schedule ⇄, pay ⇄, card line ⇄, lead ⇄, three lists ⇄ (Что делать · Кого ищем · Что предлагаем — one item per line), «Контакт». The slug is made from the English title on save (`sweet_pepper_vacancy_slug()`), so the address is Latin; Cyrillic would have percent-encoded.
- **Dates the theme keeps** (`inc/vacancies.php`): `sweet_pepper_vacancy_apply_show()` on `save_post_vacancy` at 20 — a term counts from the save; the same term re-saved changes nothing; another term counts again from today; «В архиве» closes; a term on an archived record reopens. `sweet_pepper_vacancy_sweep()` daily on WP-Cron flips expired postings to «В архиве» and purges WP Super Cache; `sweet_pepper_vacancy_is_open()` checks the date at render time too. Admin: the note under «На сайте» (open until … / in the archive since …), columns Отдел · На сайте · Контакт, views «Открытые» / «Архив». **Tested from the CLI:** same-term re-save unchanged; 2 → 4 weeks recomputed; a past date → closed at render; the sweep closed it (`show` = archive, `closed` = the date); a term reopened it; the sweep is scheduled.
- **Contacts:** Bar Settings → «Контакты» (`acf-json/group_sp_contacts.json`, `inc/contacts.php`, fallback `data/contacts.php`): the bar's phone, email, Telegram, VK, Instagram — one source, read by the vacancy page first; the Visit card, home Contacts and the footer still type them (next). The list «Кто отвечает за вакансии» (name ⇄, role ⇄, phone, Telegram, email; a stable id per row on save) feeds the record's «Контакт» dropdown (`acf/load_field`); a pick whose row is gone falls back to the bar (tested). Telegram accepts `@name`, `name` or a t.me link.
- **The page:** `single-vacancy.php` → `template-parts/vacancy/{head,body,contact-card,foot,phone-bar}.php`, `src/css/vacancy.css`; header and footer-edge rules for About/Visit extended to `.single-vacancy` (header.css, footer.css). Head: connector reflection (ROOM FOR ONE MORE / ТВОЁ МЕСТО В КОМАНДЕ via `about/connector.php`), back link, the Careers eyebrow (the About tab's own), H1, meta row. Body: copy in columns 1–7, the contact card sticky in 8–12 — **four columns were too few:** the Visit rows (handle + copy chip + verb) wrapped the phone number, seen on the first shot. Tablets: the card a band under the head, rows in two columns. Phones: card after the copy, a Call · Write bar pinned at the bottom (the footer takes 76px so its last row is not covered). Closed: head + "This role is filled…" + the foot, `noindex`; open: `JobPosting` JSON-LD. `<title>` = the role in the request's language.
- **About Careers:** `template-parts/components/position-card.php` (the card markup extracted from `about/careers.php`; also the "other open roles" row on a posting); `sweet_pepper_about_careers()` returns `sweet_pepper_vacancy_positions()` once any vacancy record exists, the repeater otherwise; the row wraps (three a row, `max-width` a third) — the "four roles" open item closed.
- **Strings:** `ru_RU.l10n.php` → Vacancy page (mine: Подробнее, Все вакансии, Открыта с %s, Вакансия закрыта…, Что делать / Кого ищем / Что предлагаем, Ваш контакт, Или в бар, Позвонить, Написать: %s, Откликнуться на hh.ru, Другие вакансии; the bar's own "Write" with a context, so the Visit card's English Write is untouched).

Neither is a new rule; `design.md` is unchanged.

A dump from before the seed sits in the session scratchpad, not in the repo.

## Menu hero photo: a true crossfade, and the stack leans with each section — 24 September 2026

Author: the side nav's easing was fixed on 18 Sep, but the photo still changed too quickly and abruptly; and the original idea — each image flips the tilt (+1 / −1) and the sheet order, as the home hero's tiles do — was never built on the menu. Both built, worktree of `wip`, verified on a private server with the worktree's theme (the Local site serves the main checkout) in headless Chrome.

- **The crossfade (`menu-hero.js`, `menu-hero.css` → Photo Stack).** The old swap was one layer: opacity down to 0.3 over 500 ms, `src` cut at 250 ms, back up — the brief had recorded it as "cuts at ≈ 40% opacity; a true crossfade needs a second image layer". Now `showSection()` clones the outgoing photo as `.menu-hero__photo-ghost` over the incoming one (the dish picker's `rollPhoto()` device), sets the new `src` underneath, waits for `decode()`, then fades the ghost 1 → 0 with Web Animations on `--ease-gentle-flat` over **`--menu-photo-dur` 1100 ms — the words' `--menu-word-dur`**, so the photo and the fill change on one clock. Ghosts stack under rapid hovers (each a snapshot of what was on show, newest lowest, at most three) and all fade when the newest photo is ready; measured: six words 60 ms apart → three ghosts, all gone by 1.2 s. The pill dips out over 300 ms, its caption changes, and it comes back (`.is-swapping` on the frame); the description changes when the new photo starts to show. Reduced motion, and the silent daypart swap during the entrance: everything changes at once, no ghost. `.is-fading` and the img's `opacity` transition are gone.
- **The desktop stack is the home tile's colour stack, ported whole (`hero.css` → Active tile colour stack).** First pass kept the menu's old desktop fan (sheets the photo's size, centred 10px right of it and shifted 4–8px right and down) as the −1° baseline; the author, on the page: *the sheets sit too far right and the Lemon hides behind the Chili — the home tiles are almost perfect, the positioning and order based on the tilt took a long time to get right.* So: a **`.menu-hero__stack`** wrapper is the tile — the photo at its top-left, an **8px pocket** right and below (was a 20px right padding on `.menu-hero__photo`; the photo is 12px wider now, 712 at 1440), the three SLOTS (`.menu-hero__photo-sheet--near / --mid / --far`, z 3 / 2 / 1) the stack's width at 3:2 — 8px wider than the photo — pinned at its top-left and fanned by rotation about their own centre with the home's shifts: `--x / --y / --a` = −6 −4 −1° · −4 −2 −1.5° · −2 0 −2°, near radius 4. **`--dir`** mirrors about the photo with the home's formula (`--sx: calc(var(--dir) * var(--x) − (1 − var(--dir)) * 4px)`, angles flip, y stays) and flips the tilt (`--tilt: calc(var(--dir) * −1deg)`): dir 1 → −1°, fan below-left; −1 → +1°, fan below-right — the sheets belong to the photo's tilt, as on the home. Measured: every slot's translate and angle equal the home tile's for both directions. **Direction alternates with the word's position in the list** (index parity — `data-dir` on the section, printed by `menu-hero.php` for the first frame and kept by `setDir()`; the CSS derives `--dir` from it; the door's preview, not in the list, leans against whatever was up), so the sweep and a hover down the list lean each photo against the last. **Colours — the home's four recipes**, chosen by the direction and the section's Figma component (Recipe B = Salads, Soups, Desserts, Beer, No Buzz): A −1° Lime · Lemon · Chili (breakfast) · A +1° Lemon · Lime · Chili (lunch) · B −1° Lime · Chili · Lemon (dinner) · B +1° Chili · Lime · Lemon (party). With the lists as they are, B sections are all odd-indexed (+1°), so B +1° = the phone's Recipe B. **Below 992px nothing changes:** the phone stack keeps its inset fan and its two recipes (Lemon · Lime · Chili / Chili · Lime · Lemon), the pocket now on the stack wrapper — rects identical to the old CSS at 834 and 402, recipes A and B. On desktop a change moves the stack: tilt and fan on the spring over `--menu-fan-dur` 900 ms (the arrow's, the entrance fan's), the sheets' colours on the flat curve at the photo's pace; gated on `.is-settled` and `(min-width: 992px)`, so the silent daypart swap does not animate. The entrance fan is the home's `hero-sheet-fan` keyframe now (`menu-sheet-fan` deleted).
- **Measured (1440 × 900, `?daypart=breakfast`, then Lunch, then Salads):** ghost 1 → 0.81 (300 ms) → 0.46 (450) → 0.07 (800) → removed at 1.2 s; frame −1° → +1.07° peak → +1° at 1 s; near sheet x +12 → −16 from the frame's left; pill 0.9 → 0 at 300 ms → 0.9 by 600; Salads: near Lemon → Chili and far Chili → Lemon crossfade over ~1 s, `--dir` stays −1 (index 3). `?daypart=lunch` at load: `--dir −1`, +1° from the first sample, no ghost, nothing transitions. Reduced motion: instant, no ghost. No console errors through the entrance.
- **Room wordmark in Russian — КУХНЯ ОТ ПЕРЦЕВ / БАР ОТ ПЕРЦЕВ** (author, same day: "change the RU connectors in the hero — I see ЕДА / НАПИТКИ"). ## VK feed built — the Russian home cards import the wall; English cards stay in the tab — 25 September 2026

The plan of 24 September, built and tested locally against a saved wall — not yet against the live wall (the key is the author's to place; see *To run*). Branch of `wip`; the Local site still serves the main checkout, so the checks ran through a CLI bootstrap that loads this checkout's code (below).

- **`inc/vk-feed.php`** — `sweet_pepper_vk_import()`: `wall.get` (POST, the key in the body, scrubbed from any VK error text), the community's own posts, 20 asked for; eligible = not a repost, not an ad, with words and a photo; the first 8 stored (five show, spares cover a hidden one). One `news` record per post, keyed `_sp_vk_id`; cover = the first photo's largest size, downloaded once per photo id into the media library (`_sp_vk_photo`), replaced when the photo changes; caption = `sweet_pepper_vk_title()` — the first line with words (leading emoji, bullets, hashtags and `[id|Name]` links stripped), the first sentence or two within 60 characters, else a word cut with an ellipsis; date = publication date, never «Today!» (`plain_date` on `event-card.php`); the card links to `vk.com/wall-64582467_ID`; VK's pinned post → the pinned card. A re-run updates by hash, never duplicates. **A caption the team retypes stays** — the importer rewrites only the one it wrote (`_sp_vk_title`). **Gone from the wall** (newer than the oldest fetched post, absent from a successful fetch): marked `_sp_vk_missing_since`, Draft on the second successful miss, republished if it comes back. **A failed request writes nothing** — the page keeps the last successful feed; `sp_vk_last_error` / `sp_vk_last_success` / `sp_vk_last_summary` show above the list in admin (no secrets). WP Super Cache purged after a change, when installed.
- **`sweet_pepper_vk_cards( 5 )`** — the RU page's cards: published records, not hidden, with a cover; pinned first, then newest. **`sweet_pepper_home_events()`** takes them on a Russian request and falls back to the tab's rows only until the first import; an English request reads the tab's rows and never the feed.
- **`news` post type** re-registered as «Посты ВКонтакте» (`inc/cpt.php`): not public (`SWEET_PEPPER_REWRITE_VERSION` → 2), no «Add new», a side box with the post's link, date and the importer's own caption, a «На сайте» column. **`acf-json/group_sp_news.json`** (generated): «Скрыть с сайта» (`news_hidden`), «Описание фото (alt)» (`news_alt`), a note. The team's two knobs; nothing else to fill.
- **The «Что нового» tab** is now the English page's: «Карточки (EN)», caption and alt English-only (the RU twins retired from `group_sp_home.json`, `tools/page-field-groups.py`, `tools/page-seed.php`), the note says where the Russian cards come from. Saved `title_ru` values stay in the database unused.
- **Schedule:** a WP-Cron event `sweet_pepper_vk_import`, hourly, scheduled on `init` once a key exists; cleared on theme switch. On hosting: `DISABLE_WP_CRON` + a system cron calling `wp-cron.php` hourly — not configured yet (the author runs server commands).
- **`tools/vk-import.php`** — `--dry-run` (what a run would do, per post: new / record / repost / no photo / no words, the caption), `--from <wall.get.json>` (a saved response instead of VK), a plain run (import, then the five RU cards). Loads this checkout's feed code when the active theme is another copy.
- **Tested (fixture wall of six posts, local database, then deleted):** create 3 / repost, wordless and photo-less skipped; re-run unchanged 3; a caption retyped by hand survives a VK edit of the same post; a post gone from the wall → missing, then Draft, then back to publish when it returned (photo reused, not re-downloaded: 3 attachments throughout); hidden record leaves the cards; the rendered card carries `event-card--pinned`, the VK mark, a plain «25 Sep», the post link. **Live run, 25 Sep (author's key in Local's `wp-config.php`):** 20 fetched, 8 eligible (one photo-less post skipped, no reposts), 8 created, the covers downloaded from VK's CDN and cropped to 720×900; a second look at the captions caught trailing emoji («🤫», «👇🏼») — now every emoji, skin tone and joiner is dropped before the cut and what they closed («вечера 👇🏼») loses its dangling colon. Not tested: the hourly event firing, WP Super Cache. **Found and fixed the same day:** `/` printed «17 Sep» — not a missing pack (ru_RU is installed) but timing: wp-settings.php loads WordPress's own strings and builds `WP_Locale` (month names) for the SITE language before functions.php can say the request's; `inc/lang.php` now reloads the default text domain for the request's locale at `after_setup_theme` and rebuilds `WP_Locale` — «17 Сен» on `/`, «17 Sep» on `/en/`, the theme's strings unaffected. Any date the site prints through `date_i18n` / `get_the_date` follows.
- **To run:** `define( 'SWEET_PEPPER_VK_SERVICE_KEY', '…' )` in Local's `wp-config.php` (the author's own step — the key never enters the repository), then `"$PHP" -d mysqli.default_socket="$SOCK" tools/vk-import.php --dry-run`, then without `--dry-run`. The five cards then need the author's look on `/` (photo choice for multi-photo posts — the first photo today — the 4:5 crop, the caption cuts). **Docs:** `website-brief.md` → News/social feed (build status, next steps renumbered), `testing.md` → VK home feed (rows updated).

Author: the team cannot duplicate daily social publishing in WordPress; English needs an independent manual portfolio feed without translation work. **Decision:** automatic VK previews on `/`, manual cards on `/en/`. VK publication is the RU approval step; the earlier “import as drafts, then approve in WordPress” plan is superseded. About/HH is outside this home-feed change.

- **Plan recorded, not built:** hourly server-side imports, five eligible original photo posts, local media-library covers, shortened opening text, publication dates and direct VK post links. No inferred event dates/categories. Deduplicate by source IDs, update source edits, preserve hides/overrides, retain the last successful feed on failure. Validate multi-photo selection/crops and source-deletion handling before unattended operation. No English fallback to Russian cards.
- **Current build corrected:** Home now reads SCF `home_event_cards` through `inc/home-data.php`, with typed fallbacks; the rows still share photos/date/source/link across languages. `news` is registered but not connected to these cards. Independent feeds, imports and scheduling remain to build.
- **API verified:** community `64582467`; mini-app **service key**, `wall.get` API `5.199`, owner filter, ten posts. Local test returned ten, nine with photos/text. The author's Timeweb PHP cURL test returned **SUCCESS: received 10 posts from VK.** No posts or site content changed; photo downloads, WordPress integration and cron are not tested.
- **Key rotation:** the first tested key appeared in a screenshot; the author supplied a replacement, which passed the local ten-post test. Old-key revocation not independently verified. Replacement remains in a private temporary local file outside Git; it is not installed or tested on Timeweb yet. No credentials recorded here.
- **Next:** local importer + RU/EN separation → real-image download test and five-preview review → test-site deploy and private replacement-key configuration → failure/edit/delete/duplicate/EN/cache checks → verified hourly hosting schedule. Full operating plan: `website-brief.md` → *News/social feed*; checks: `testing.md` → *VK home feed*.
- **This session changes documentation only.** No importer, hosting configuration or scheduler has been built or deployed.

## The home page in admin — «Главная», one tab per section — 25 September 2026

Author: "build the Home page in wp-admin with SCF fields, the same way we did for the About and menu pages." Built on the branch `claude/home-page-scf-fields-06f88c` (cut from `wip` a29e44e) while the main checkout carried the parallel session's Visit-page extraction; **merged into `wip` the same day** (author: "merge and commit everything") — the Visit work committed first as found, then the branch, conflicts resolved as the list at the end expected, `dist/` rebuilt, `page-seed.php home` run: **page 2876 «Главная» is the front page now** (Settings → Reading), `/` and `/en/` open it on the live local site.

- **The page.** The home page had no page: Settings → Reading opened the site on the posts index and `front-page.php` rendered by name, so there was nothing for fields to sit on. **`tools/page-seed.php home`** makes «Главная» (slug `home`, no template — `front-page.php` wins whatever the template) and sets it as the static front page; `group_sp_home` is located on *page type = front page*, whichever page that is. **`/en/` needed one line:** WordPress turns an *empty* home query into the static front page, and the English twin rule carried `sp_lang=en` — one extra query var, and `/en/` would have stayed on the posts index and rendered `index.php`. `inc/lang.php` now drops `sp_lang` from the parsed query (`request` filter; nothing read it — the language comes from the URI). Checked from the CLI: `/` and `/en/` → `front-page.php`, page 2876; `/home/` → `/`, `/en/home/` → `/en/`, `/en` → `/en/` (WordPress's own canonical redirects).
- **Fields** (`tools/page-field-groups.py` → `group_sp_home.json`, prefix `field_sp_home_`, 169 fields, eight tabs in page order, labels in Russian): **«Первый экран»** — eyebrow ⇄; per daypart (Завтрак · Обед · Ужин · Вечер) the tile photo (3:2) + alt ⇄, headline ⇄, body ⇄, the menu button's word ⇄; the three closed-hours sets (night / morning / Sunday) headline ⇄ + body ⇄, with **`{time}`** for the opening time the engine fills in from Bar Settings. **«Что попробовать»** — header; exactly three cards, each **a Relationship to one dish or drink** (name, price, photo from its record, the link to its section — *Menu storage → Settled*) with the card's own short name ⇄ and line ⇄ (the copy doc's lines are shorter than the menu rows'; the picker's «Короткое имя на билете» is the precedent), or — with no dish — a category typed by hand (photo, price ⇄ «От 150 ₽», description ⇄, section select; shown only while no dish is picked); tag icon (select, the dish legend) + word ⇄. **«Настойки» / «Обед»** — title ⇄, photo (3:2) + alt ⇄, caption ⇄, **a Relationship of three drinks / dishes** (every row highlighted: the preview's picks). **«О баре»** — header, photo + alt ⇄, **the founding year** (a number; «12 лет» / "12 years" is counted by `sweet_pepper_years_since()`, the tenure rule), the rating ⇄. **«Что нового»** — header; cards repeater (max 5: cover 4:5, caption ⇄, date, label select, source, link, «Закрепить», alt ⇄); the «Ещё во ВКонтакте» tile (photo, words ⇄, link). **«Визит и связь»** — header + the phone paragraph ⇄, the map's heading ⇄, «Перед визитом» heading + text ⇄. **«Поиск»** — tab title ⇄ (`document_title_parts`, the site name after it) + meta description ⇄. Structure stays in code: the tile order, the daypart clock and theme, the button's glyph and destination, the connectors, the phone / VK / Instagram / address / map, the Follow buttons' links.
- **Readers and templates.** `inc/home-data.php` (new): `sweet_pepper_home_page()` (from Settings → Reading; null → the typed copy), `_hero()`, `_highlights()`, `_preview( 'bar' | 'kitchen' )`, `_about()`, `_events()`, `_contacts()`, the title and meta hooks. `sweet_pepper_page_rows()` / `sweet_pepper_page_text()` — About's row and prose helpers — moved to `inc/fields.php` and renamed (About calls them). `sweet_pepper_menu_item_row()` (a post → the row shape) and `sweet_pepper_menu_dish_args()` (a row → dish-row args) factored out of the menu readers so the previews print a store item exactly as the menu does — the four menu / About renders diffed byte-identical but for whitespace. **`front-page.php` → seven parts in `template-parts/home/`**, the About shape, `$home_id` resolved once; typed fallback and seed source **`data/home/<section>.php`** (English as typed; Russian from `home-copy-ru-draft.md`, the rest marked `// draft`).
- **The daypart engine reads the words from the page.** `template-parts/home/hero.php` prints the four sets and the closed lines as JSON (`.home-hero__data`, one language); `daypart-engine.js` merges them over its typed dictionary, which stays the fallback and the home of what is code (mode, `btnIcon`, `btnHref`). `{time}` (or `{время}`) → the opening time. The `?hl=` "now word" prototype is untouched (RU headlines carry no `now`, so nothing lights).
- **UI strings** on the home page wrapped in `__()` and given Russian (`ru_RU.l10n.php`, from the RU draft where it has them): Now, Reserve, Drinks / Food menu, Explore the … menu, Read the full story, See what's on VK, View Instagram, VK message / Instagram DM (the two drawers too), Usually answer in 20 minutes *(mine — the RU review asks not to promise a time)*, Copy / Copied / Copy address / Address copied, Directions *(with a context: the Visit page's own "Directions" means «К карте»)*, Yandex Maps, Plan your visit, the address, the social cards' pills (Событие · Акция · Наши гости — mine), See it on VK / Instagram, Today!. **The language nudge** now speaks the *other* language and is a link to this page in it («Prefer English? Switch to English →» on `/`, the Russian line on `/en/`) — it was one fixed Russian line with no link.
- **Photos** the team may replace: the four tiles, the two preview photos, the About photo, the social covers and the tile (a new hard-cropped size **`sp-4x5`** 720×900, `inc/images.php`); `inc/admin-photo-preview.php` previews them at 3:2 / 4:5 by name. Seeded as attachments: 19 new. **Two dishes got their «Фото»** from the cards' typed photos — Крылышки-гриль (354) and Жаркое по-ярославски (385) had none, so the cards (and the menu's strip) would have shown their sections' hero photos.

The doc's lines are the price of one record per dish; to have them on the home page, put them on the dish. The highlight cards keep the doc's lines as the card's own line.
- **Verified:** CLI renders with the worktree theme against the Local database (`/`, `/en/` unseeded and seeded; zero notices): the English page differs from the committed render only in the title / meta, the nudge link, the JSON block, escaping and whitespace; seeded, only in what the store answers. A private `php -S` on :8089 serving the worktree's theme with the home page as the front page (option filters, no touch of Local's symlink) + headless Chrome: **`/` and `/en/` at 1440 and 402**, `?closed=night|morning|sunday` in Russian (ДОБРОЙ НОЧИ · До встречи за завтраком в 8:30 · УЖЕ НА НОГАХ? · ВОСКРЕСНАЯ УБОРКА! … с 10) and English, `?daypart=party`; the button's word and href follow the daypart in both languages; no console errors, no horizontal overflow; the phone's «Написать в ВК · Написать в Instagram» pair fits the two-up row (177px each, one line). Shots eyeballed. **Found:** the third RU card, «ЖАРКОЕ ПО-ЯРОСЛАВСКИ», wraps its price to a second row — the brief's open card-row alignment (Mobile — Highlights → Open), one word longer in Russian. **Not seen: the admin screens** (no login from the CLI).

Author: "there is a photo for the menu sections that is supposed to be 4:1. Is it correct that we can't show this ratio in the WordPress admin panel?" — it isn't; option 2 of the two offered (a live crop in the field rather than an overlay on the full photo) chosen and built.

- **`inc/admin-photo-preview.php`** (new; `acf/input/admin_head` + `admin_footer`, only on page / dish / drink edit screens). Fields are found by name from SCF's `data-name` wrapper attribute, so regenerated groups need nothing: **`sec_<slug>_photo`** → the preview takes the 4:1 shape and the photo slides under it with **`sec_<slug>_photo_y`**, `object-position: 50% Y%` — the same rule `reveal.css` applies on the site; **`sec_<slug>_hero_photo`** keeps its 3:2 frame and gets the tablet's 21:9 window drawn over it (64.29% of the height, dimmed outside, Lemon outline, «планшет» label) moving with **`sec_<slug>_hero_focus`** — the desktop shows the whole 3:2, so the overlay is the honest form here; **`photo`** (dish / drink), **`menu_door_photo`**, **`menu_door_night_photo`** → a 3:2 centre crop. A one-line note under each preview says what it shows. The range and number inputs both drive it (`input` / `change`); `acf.addAction('ready' / 'append' / 'change')` re-scans after a newly chosen photo or an added repeater row.

- **Sliders default to 50** (found the same morning from the author's screenshot): a range is never empty, so an unsaved slider showed 0 and the first Update would have stored the top of every photo. Regenerated, re-imported; nothing had been saved at 0.
- **Verified** on a mock of SCF's field markup (from the plugin source: `.acf-field[data-name]`, `.acf-image-uploader .image-wrap img`, `.acf-range-wrap input`) with the module's own CSS and script in headless Chrome: ratios 4.00 / 1.50 / 1.50, the band's object-position follows the slider (80% → 10%), the hero window moves 22px → 99px of 307 for 20 → 90, notes present; screenshot eyeballed. **Not seen on the real edit screen** (no login from the CLI) — the author's first look.

Typed fallback `data/menu/page.php` → `door_night`: the Manhattan (`bar/cocktails/manhattan-3-2.jpg`, the Cocktails hero photo), caption «Манхэттен» / "Manhattan", paragraphs drafted RU + EN (marked `// draft`). Seeded by `page-seed.php menu`.

- **Incident, fixed:** `.gitignore` had `node_modules/`, which ignores a directory but not a symlink — the worktree's link to the main checkout's `node_modules` was committed with `git add -A`, and the fast-forward in the main checkout replaced its real directory with a link to itself (`vite: too many levels of symbolic links`). Untracked, `.gitignore` → `node_modules`, `npm ci` in the main checkout restored the packages.

## Menu pages in admin — stale groups, the dish photo as a field, the bar door at night — 24 September 2026

Author, from the admin screens: "I still see the last fields" (the tabs Первый экран · Подборка · Поиск under the section tabs); "where are the Seasonal menu photos exactly? I couldn't find them in the Dishes"; «Блюда подборки» → «Блюда сезонного меню»; and does any doc say the bar door should show a different photo in the night food menu, cocktails for instance?

- **The extra tabs were database copies.** SCF's *Sync available* had copied «Меню — страница» (`group_sp_menu`) and «Раздел меню — списки блюд» (`group_sp_menu_list`) into the database before their JSON files were retired, and a database group outlives its file. Trashed both (IDs 640, 305). Lesson for the deploy routine: **retiring a group's file is not enough where the group was synced — delete it in SCF too** (added to the test-site steps). The three current groups were re-imported from their JSON so the label change shows without another sync.
- **The dish photo is now a «Фото» field, first on the dish form** (`group_sp_dish.json`, 3:2 note under it). The photo had been the post's featured image — a sidebar box the author did not find. `sweet_pepper_dish_sync_photo()` (`inc/menu-data-dishes.php`, `acf/save_post`) copies the field into `_thumbnail_id` on every save, so the pickers' thumbnails, the tables' «Фото» column and the seasonal strip keep reading the featured image and never diverge from the form; `dish` / `drink` lost `thumbnail` support, so the sidebar box is gone. Checked by CLI: set → mirrored, cleared → cleared; the pages render byte-identical.
- **«Блюда сезонного меню» / «Напитки сезонного меню»** — the picker's label on each page (`tools/page-field-groups.py`).
- **The bar door at night — not in the docs.** Nothing decides it. What the docs do say: the hero's idle opening frame for the bar follows the daypart (brief → Menu page → Hero → Idle behaviour: tea & coffee in the morning … cocktails late), and the Figma door component had a `drinksCoffee` state that was never routed (Jump-nav on phones), which is why the bar door's typed preview is the cappuccino — the coffee state. **Recorded as open** in the brief (Menu page fields → Open) with two ways: (a) a second photo + caption + paragraph on the door tab for the night theme; (b) no new field — the door previews the bar's daypart section (its tab's hero photo, caption and paragraph), the same answer the idle sweep already gives, so coffee by day and cocktails late without anyone maintaining a second set.

## Two menu pages and their fields — the door, Highlights as references, the title — 24 September 2026

**Second pass, same day (author, from the admin screens): `/menu/food/` + `/menu/bar/` under a folder.** "Menu looks like a parent page — is it supposed to be the food menu?" So: the kitchen page moved to **`/menu/food/`** («Меню — кухня») and **`/menu/` is now a folder page «Меню»** (template *Menu — Index*, `page-menu-index.php`) whose only job is a 301 to the kitchen — or to the bar for the old `?menu=drinks`; WordPress needs the parent for the child URLs, and the team sees both menus under one folder. The address bar says which menu it is. `tools/page-seed.php menu` does the move in place (renames the kitchen page's slug, creates the folder, re-parents both children; idempotent — the same command on the test site), so page 8 kept its fields. The folder's edit screen carries one message (`group_sp_menu_index`) saying it holds nothing. **Gotcha met while migrating locally:** `wp_update_post()` re-validates a page's template against the *served* theme's files and silently resets an unknown one to "default" — the bar page lost `page-menu-bar.php` because the Local site still served the main checkout; the seeder now re-asserts the template by meta after every update. Also trashed the lingering «Раздел меню» group (option А, `group_sp_menu_section`, ID 278) that SCF kept re-exporting into `acf-json/`. Every «Menu» link — header, footer, drawer, the About and Visit CTAs, the five home-page links, the home hero's daypart button — goes straight to `/menu/food/` through `sweet_pepper_menu_url()`, so a guest never takes the hop; only old links and bookmarks do. **Found and fixed on the way:** the header and drawer marked "Menu" current with `is_page_template( 'page-menu.php' )`, so the bar page had lost the mark — now `sweet_pepper_is_menu_page()`. Also merged today: the parallel session's 23 Sep work was committed as found in the main checkout and merged into this branch (`highlights.php` resolved to the reader, which already carried its decisions; `report.md` keeps both sessions' entries).

**Where the menu's words live now, all three tiers:** a section's words on its «Разделы меню» record (23 Sep), a dish's on its «Блюда» / «Напитки» post, and — from today — a page's on the page. The author's question opened it: "where do the fields go, «Разделы меню» or pages?" — pages, and "two pages in admin, food and drinks?" There was one: «Menu» (ID 8, `page-menu.php`) rendering both states from `?menu=drinks`. Now there are two.

- **Two pages, the state is the template** (author's preference; the technical case: a page cache never caches a query string, so the bar menu was uncached; each page gets its own `<title>`, canonical, hreflang, revisions and edit lock). *(First shape, superseded the same day — see above: the kitchen stayed at `/menu/`.)* `page-menu.php` (Menu) is the kitchen; new `page-menu-bar.php` (Menu — Bar) is the bar; both render one body, `template-parts/menu-page.php`, with the state as its arg — `page-menu.php` lost its 150 lines to it. `inc/menu-page.php` (new): `sweet_pepper_menu_state()` from the queried page's template, `sweet_pepper_menu_page( $state )` found by template (the team may rename the page), `sweet_pepper_menu_url( $state, $section )` — a permalink, so `/en/` rides along. **The bar page: «Меню — бар», slug `bar` under Menu → `/menu/bar/`** (local: page 601, made by the seeder). **`/menu/?menu=drinks` is a 301** to it, in the request's language, the `#section` hash surviving as browsers keep it; until the bar page exists (a fresh site, the test site before its seed) the kitchen template still renders the bar for that URL, so nothing ever 404s. Nine links moved to `sweet_pepper_menu_url()` (home page ×3, hero door, jump-nav, dish picker CTA in PHP and JS, the home hero's party button in `daypart-engine.js`); `inc/daypart-head.php` prints the bar page as night before paint from the template, and the engine reads WordPress's `page-template-page-menu-bar` body class — `?menu=drinks` stays as a checker's switch on any page. The RU connector swap (`sweet_pepper_menu_connector_lang`) asks the page's state, not `$_GET`.
- **`group_sp_menu` «Меню — страница»** — generated in `tools/page-field-groups.py` (prefix `field_sp_mpage_`), located on both templates, three tabs: **«Первый экран»** — the door to the other menu: word ⇄, hover photo (3:2), caption ⇄, paragraph ⇄ (the hero's `__()` door strings are gone; `menu-hero.php` reads `sweet_pepper_menu_door()`); **«Подборка»** — eyebrow ⇄, headline line 1 ⇄, line 2 ⇄, and **a Relationship list of dishes or drinks (max 8, thumbnails in the picker, the same size · price · description label as the section lists')** — a card's name, photo and section link come from the dish's record, never copied (the brief's *surfaces reference dishes*); **«Поиск»** — the page's name in the browser tab ⇄ (WordPress adds the site name; `document_title_parts`) and a meta description ⇄ (`wp_head`) — the first page with a real need for the `<title>` twin the brief listed as not built. What stays in code: the FOOD / DRINKS wordmark, «Ещё блюда / Ещё напитки», the connectors, the default section. The pairing-station heading (`.po`), the Location text and the phone Visit CTA (About's fields, 23 Sep) already had homes — no tab for them.
- **Highlights never blank — three sources in order** (`sweet_pepper_menu_highlights()`): the page's list → while it is empty, **every dish (drink on the bar page) of the menu carrying a seasonal label, in menu order, eight at most** — the "deliberately wrong" option (no field, the label decides) kept as the fallback because the strip is a curated six in a chosen order → while the store has none, the typed cards in `data/menu/page.php` (the old six). **A card's photo is the dish's featured image**, else the photo of the section that places it — the author's concern ("these cards show seasonal dishes, not the section image") answered by the featured image: `dish` / `drink` always supported one; now the box is titled «Фото блюда» / «Фото напитка» with a 3:2 note under it, and the Dishes / Drinks tables open with a «Фото» column so the team sees who has none (nobody yet: 0 of 253). A card links to its section — an anchor on its own page, a URL to the other page. **RU card link = the section's nav label alone, and the RU heading is one line** — the author's 23 Sep decisions found in a parallel session's uncommitted `highlights.php` («Салаты от Перцев» overflows a 262px card), carried into the reader; the strip's heading goes through `sp_headline()` so the empty RU line 2 never borrows «Highlights».
- **Seeded locally** (`tools/page-seed.php menu`; DB backed up first): the bar page created; on both pages the door (photos into the media library), the header, the title and description, and the list = what the empty list shows anyway — 5 seasonal dishes (Летний салат с брынзой, Летний супчик дня, Фетучини Корфу, Жаркое с треской, Равиоли) and 7 seasonal drinks (Ледяная лимонная, Лилиан, Эммануэль, Гемини, Сырный, Сезонный лимонад, Сырный раф) — so the form and the page agree. **Gazpacho and Okroshka**, two of the old six cards, are not in the store (never in `menu.md`), so they left the strip; Caramel cheesecake and Summer salad are in it without a seasonal label — add the label or pick them in «Подборка».
- **Verified** (worktree theme rendered from the CLI against the Local DB, both languages, both pages): against the committed `wip` tree the kitchen page differs only in title, meta, the door's href and the picker CTA's href (plus the cards); the bar page additionally in its own canonical / hreflang / body class / shortlink; `?menu=drinks` → 301 `/menu/bar/`, `/en/menu/?menu=drinks#cocktails` → 301 `/en/menu/bar/`; zero PHP notices; the group loads with 24 fields, the picker's search returns «Фетучини Корфу — 250 г · 465-. · …»; `php -l` and div nesting on every changed template. **Not seen: the admin screens** — no login from the CLI. **Not carried to the main checkout:** it holds ~30 files of another session's uncommitted work, `highlights.php` and `menu-sections.php` among them, so the patch would have clobbered live edits — the author merges this branch instead. Until then the local site's `/menu/bar/` (page 601 exists in the shared DB) renders with `index.php`.
- **Drafts for the author (RU):** the door paragraphs, the door caption «Капучино с мороженым», the eyebrow «вкусно и свежо», both pages' «Поиск» title + description — marked `// draft` in `data/menu/page.php`. EN card links still say "Show in {label}" (the copy doc's "See in" was never propagated).
- **Test site after deploy:** SCF → *Sync available* («Меню — страница»), then `cd ~/repository-test && WP_ROOT=~/SweetPepper-test/public_html php tools/page-seed.php menu` — it creates the bar page there. Then check `/menu/?menu=drinks` redirects.

## Cyrillic headline room — Й and Ё under the eyebrow — 23 September 2026

Author, on the Russian menu: "we need a bigger gap between the section headline and the brow because of the й letter" — and, choosing the fix, "for every headline, it should be consistent". Rule in `design.md` → §3.3 *Cyrillic*.
- **Measured:** Molot's breve (Й) and dots (Ё) rise 0.245em above the capitals — 15.7 / 11.8 / 8.8px at 64 / 48 / 36.
- **Built:** `components.css` → *Cyrillic*: `:lang(ru)` + eyebrow-and-headline sibling pairs get `padding-top: 0.25em` (of the headline's own size) — section-header (Home, About, Menu highlights), the menu's phone closer, Home hero, About hero, Visit hero, Visit location; Location (About, Menu) `calc(var(--text-h1) * .25)` because its size lives on the line spans; menu section titles ≥ 992 only (below it the headline is a rail word and the eyebrow sits under it). `about.css`: About's Story and Guests titles flow inline on phones (and Story on tablets) — the room goes on the group there. `hero.css`: in Russian the home tiles give the 0.25 × Display back (`--tile` … `100vh − 612px − 0.25 × --text-display`), so the hero keeps its height where the tile is height-bound. Rebuilt.
- **Verified** (reduced motion, so nothing is parked by the reveal engine — parked, the numbers double, because a headline is parked by its own height): every RU eyebrow → headline gap is English + 16 / 12 / 9px at 1440 / 768 / 402 (+19 for the 76px heroes); within a width every RU section reads the same (20 / 17 / 14); every English gap unchanged (+0). Home and About heroes fit the viewport at 1440×900, 1280×720, 402×874, 375×667 in both languages; at 1440×800 RU and EN heroes are the same height.
- **Open (author):** RU home hero at **1180×690** ends 7px past the viewport (EN 12px inside) — the tile is at its 168 floor there. Options: a Russian floor of 152 (4px grid), or accept it for that size. 1024×650 overflows in both languages already (not new). Figma: the RU frames need the same +0.25em (design.md §9).

## Media topics — labels instead of folders — 23 September 2026

Author: "is there a way to organise the media library — folders by page/theme?" WordPress has none; a folders plugin would spend the free plugin slot on an admin nicety, so the theme adds **labels** (`website-brief.md` → Platform → *Media topics*).
- **`inc/media-topics.php`** (new, required from `functions.php`): taxonomy `media_topic` on attachments — hierarchical (checkboxes, not a tag box), not public, `query_var` `media_topic`, admin column on, generic term count (attachments are `inherit`). Default topics **Еда · Бар · Команда · Гости · События · Интерьер · Логотип и бренд** made on the first admin load (`sp_media_topics_seeded` option — once, so a deleted topic stays deleted); Media → Темы to rename or add.
- **Where the team meets it:** «Тема» checkboxes in an image's details (`attachment_fields_to_edit` / `_save`; WordPress's own comma-separated slug box removed); a «Все темы» filter in the grid toolbar and every picker (`wp_enqueue_media` → an `AttachmentFilters` subclass added to `AttachmentsBrowser`'s toolbar; the attachment AJAX query passes the taxonomy's query var itself); a list-view dropdown with counts; list-view bulk actions «Тема: …» (added, not replaced) and «Тема: снять все».
- **`tools/media-topics.php`** (new): labels unlabelled images by the field they sit in — keys matched whole, never by value, because an attachment ID can equal a price (the survey found 65 = Ararat's ID = a dish price). Locally all 35: Еда 6 · Бар 6 · Команда 14 · Гости 8 · бренд 1.
- **Verified (CLI):** the `media_topic=bar` query returns the six drink photos; checkboxes tick / untick; bulk add; list dropdown. **Not seen:** the grid / picker filter — media-views JS, needs a wp-admin login.
- **Deferred by the author:** image position (focal point) — after the menu's section content moves into «Разделы меню»; plan in the brief (a *Фото* field + a vertical slider driving `--img-y`).

## Deploy: field changes travel as a file — 22 September 2026

SCF values stay in each site's database, so today's About edits (hero headline split into two fields, the RU invitation copy, the author's seven RU edits) would not reach the test site with the theme. `tools/field-update.php <file>` applies a dated set — `tools/field-updates/2026-09-22-about-ru.json` — group by group, all-or-nothing, and only where every field still holds its expected old value; anything edited in admin since is SKIPPED and printed, a group already applied reports "done" (run twice locally: all "done"; a wrong old value: SKIPPED, field untouched). Keys follow the generator (`field_sp_` + name), so it also writes the new `about_hero_headline_2_*` before anyone has saved the tab. Also: `russian-website-voice.md` brought up to date (all nine About connectors on the page; «Перец» confirmed as the bar's short name). Left out of git: `Sweet posts examples.pdf` (source of the voice guide — contains private trainee feedback).

**28 Sep 2026 — image fields:** a value written `asset:<theme path>` (e.g. `asset:bar/wine/red-2.jpg`) compares the attachment's `_sp_source` and finds or uploads the new photo from the theme, since attachment IDs differ between installs; the file's optional `uploads` map gives an upload its RU alt and «Тема». First use: `tools/field-updates/2026-09-28-gastrobot-photos.json`.

## Perks stamps — hover and pressed — 22 September 2026

`about.css` → stamp. **Hover** (hover devices): leans 8° (was a half-straighten to 2°, which read as less motion), rises 6px (`--lift`, composed in the one transform: translateY · rotate · scale), label takes the ring colour, and a glow in the stamp's own colour — the dark-card hover (`0 0 16px` at 50%) with `color-mix(… var(--stamp) 50%, transparent)` where cards use Lime; glow ≥768 only (the phone layout draws the ring on the icon). The selected stamp stays upright and keeps its own glow, it only rises. **Pressed**: the dark-card recipe — border solid in the stamp colour + `inset 2px -2px 8px` at 25% — plus the stamp goes flat, drops its lift, 0.94; label in the ring colour; on phones the same lands on the icon ring. A `:not(.is-active):active` twin matches the hover rule's weight (first pass: pressing a hovered stamp kept the 8° and the glow). Verified by computed style at 1440 and 390. Note: the inset shadow is near-invisible on Peppercorn with a transparent stamp — the solid ring and the scale carry the press.

## Language on the URL — `/` Russian, `/en/` English — 21 September 2026

The routing half of the one-document model (`website-brief.md` → Content editing → Language model — the *what* and the checks are there). One file, `inc/lang.php`, no plugin:
- **`sweet_pepper_lang()` moved here from `inc/fields.php`** and reads the request URI (`/en/` or `/en` after the home path → `en`, else `ru`) — it has to answer before WordPress parses the request, because the locale is set first. `SWEET_PEPPER_DEFAULT_LANG = 'ru'` is the unprefixed URL; `SWEET_PEPPER_LANGS` maps codes to locales. Outside a request (CLI, cron) it is the default.
- **Rewrite twins** via `rewrite_rules_array`: `en/` + every rule, query + `&sp_lang=en`; `^en/?$` → the home. 337 rules in the database now, 169 of them twins. **Flushed by version**, not by hand: `init` compares `sweet_pepper_rewrite_version` with the constant and flushes once — bump the constant when the rules change.
- **`locale`** → `ru_RU` / `en_US` on the front end (admin, ajax and CLI keep the site language). No `ru_RU` core pack is installed, so nothing but `<html lang>` changes yet.
- **`home_url`** → `/en` in front, on an English request, for everything except `wp-json` / `wp-admin` / `wp-login` / `wp-content` / `wp-includes` / `xmlrpc`; the nine typed `/menu/…` and `/about` links in `front-page.php` now go through `home_url()`; the daypart engine's four `btnHref`s get `window.spLang.root` in front (printed in `inc/daypart-head.php` beside `spBar`).
- **hreflang** ru / en / x-default in `wp_head`; WordPress's own canonical follows the filtered permalink, so `/en/about/` is canonical to itself.
- **`sweet_pepper_lang_switch()`** replaces the two dead `<button>` pairs (header, drawer): the active code is a `<span aria-current>`, the other an `<a hreflang>` to the twin URL; `header.css` drops `cursor: pointer` and adds `display: inline-block; text-decoration: none` so the box is unchanged (checked, 1440, both languages).
- **Verified:** status codes, redirects, 404, both feeds and the news archive, link prefixes on home / About / Menu, `<html lang>`, hreflang, canonical, the pill's targets, Careers + Location + Soups in Russian on `/` and English on `/en/`, oEmbed / REST unprefixed, CLI context unchanged (`home_url` unfiltered, lang `ru`).
- **Owed:** the `navigator.language` first-visit nudge; page `<title>` twins; a `ru_RU` `.po` for the `__()` strings; the test site's `sweet_pepper_rewrite_version` option flushes itself on the first request after the sync.

## Test-site round — reflection widths, drawer type, Shake It!, How it feels — 21 September 2026

Five findings from the author's phone and iPad on test.sweetpepper.bar.
- **Reflections narrower / wider than their word (home, day, iPhone + iPad).** Not layout (boxes identical, 370.0 at 402), not the files (ink fills each viewBox; server copies byte-identical), not reproducible in Chrome at any width or pixel ratio — a WebKit paint fault, amplified by the format: a ~14:1 SVG fitted with *meet* loses ~14px of width per pixel of missing height. Forcing the reflection's box 0.86px short in Chrome draws it at 357px from x=22 — the iPhone screenshot's numbers. Day only because the day word's viewBox is 2 wider than its reflection's (2px vs 1px stroke) so the two err differently; night pairs share a viewBox. **Fix:** `preserveAspectRatio="none"` on the root of all 93 files in `assets/sectionLinks/` — the word always spans the box; verified in Chrome that a short box now paints the full 370. **Owed: the author's eyes on the phone** (private tab — the SVG URLs are unversioned); re-add the attribute after any Figma re-export (`website-brief.md` → Section connectors).
- **Drawer descriptions:** Body 16 / 0% from 768 (`mobile-drawer.css`), Caption stays on phones; icon pad 7 → 9 with the taller line. Supersedes the 20 Sep "every drawer width"; noted beside `design.md` §3.3.
- **Shake It! 64 / Symbol 32 in the 768–991 band** (`dish-picker.css`): size and icon are now `--shake-size` / `--shake-icon`, the centring offsets read them. Measured on About and Menu: 48 / 40 / 24 at 402 and 1440, 64 / 56 / 32 at 768 and 991, dead centre on the photos' seam in all eight.
- **How it feels, quote rail (< 992):** neighbours showed as slivers under short quotes. `sizeViewport()` sets `--quote-slot` (viewport height − 8) on the track and `.about-quote-card__wrap` takes it as `min-height`; with every quote centred in turn the nearest neighbour stays 24px outside at 402, 375 and 834.
- **How it feels, phone cloud:** words placed by the spiral's inset-0 fallback orbited past `overflow: hidden` (PERFECT −3.1px at 402; four words at 375). Phones now `overflow: visible`.
- **How it feels, second pass the same day (author): the chosen word centres on phones, inactive words at 50%.** The desktop commit switched on as-is collided on nearly every tap (WELCOMING × INVITING 148×9) — the 380px field was 67–76% full. `how-it-feels.js`: phone field height = 1.7 × the words' gapped area ÷ width (`FIELD_AREA_PHONE`; 435 / 469 / 490 at 402 / 375 / 360, and the 360 rest-state pile is gone); `commitField()` now tries a chain (full centre, margin 12 → 4, then 60% / 30% of the way, then none) over a `solveCommit()` that clamps no tighter than a word's home and asks no pair for more gap than it rests with; phones calm the orbit to a third while a word is chosen and cap each rest orbit at half the nearest gap. Tap-tested all 16 words at 402 / 375 / 360: no overlaps; rest: ≤ 2px of box over 12 s. `about.css`: `.has-focus` dim 0.3 → 0.5, every width. **Cost:** cloud + card no longer fit one screen on 375×667 / 360×740 (701 of 591, 722 of 664) — flagged in the brief. Parameters came from a Node port of the placement + commit maths swept over 288–398px, not from rebuild-and-tap.
- **How it feels, third pass (author: fewer words on phones):** the simulation says width, not count, costs height — the four small words are filler, the four widest mids (WONDERFUL, CHARMING, PLEASANT, MAGNETIC) are the saving: field 380 / 410 / 429 at 402 / 375 / 360 (sixteen words: 435 / 469 / 490). `how-it-feels.php`: `'phone' => false` per word → `data-phone="off"` on the word and on its quotes; `how-it-feels.js` removes those nodes at load below 768 (12 words / 16 quotes), `FIELD_AREA_PHONE` 1.7 → 2.0 for this list, and `measureAndPlace()` grows the phone field 20px at a time if a word can't be seated. Float range back to 7–12px per word (it had dropped to ~1.5px on touching pairs under the neighbour cap — the author saw it). Fits one screen at 360×740 (661 of 664), not at 375×667 (642 of 591; ten words would). Tried and dropped: a runtime search over heights and spiral start angles — 60–250 ms on this Mac, a second on a phone. The sweep lives in `tools/how-it-feels-field-sim.mjs`.

## Menu storage — the dishes store, built for Soups — 20 September 2026

Second half of the brief's Soups test (`website-brief.md` → Content editing → Open → *Menu storage*; `testing.md`). Built as the **hybrid** — `dish` posts placed by ordered Relationship lists — because it contains the plain-CPT candidate: the «Б - Блюда» table *is* that screen. Both stores hold Soups at once and are independent; the decision is still the manager session's.
- **`dish` post type reworked** (`inc/cpt.php`): Russian labels («Б - Блюда»), **not public** (it had `public` + `has_archive` — live `/dish/` URLs with no template), revisions on; title = the Russian name, **Draft = off the site**, post ID = the stable id. **The Categories / Dietary Tags taxonomies are removed** — nothing read them and neither store uses them (section = the list, dietary = the icons field); they would have been two English no-op boxes on the form under test.
- **`menu_list` post type** («Б - Меню Макет») — `menu_section`'s twin: one record per section, slug = section slug, not public, revisions, admin-only creation. A separate type rather than a second group on `menu_section`, so both stores can hold Soups and location rules stay `post_type ==` (no database IDs in `acf-json/`).
- **Field groups:** `tools/menu-field-group.py` now writes three — the dish fields are defined once (`dish_fields()`) and used by the repeater row and by `group_sp_dish.json`, so the test compares structure, not forms; a post drops the three fields it has of its own (RU name, hide switch, id). `group_sp_menu_list.json`: subsections (same header fields) → a Relationship field, search filter only, returns IDs. `group_sp_menu_section.json` byte-identical (checked by diff).
- **`inc/menu-data-dishes.php`** (new): `sweet_pepper_menu_list_rows( $slug )` hands back rows **in the repeater's shape**, so `sweet_pepper_menu_subsections()` renders both stores through its one loop. Also: the Relationship picker and the «Б - Блюда» table show `220 г · 255-.` beside the name (Soups has two «Тыквенный суп» and two «Грибная кружка» — indistinguishable otherwise); table columns «Выход и цена» and «Раздел меню» (linked; «— не в меню» for a dish no list places — the hybrid's two-step add made visible), sorted A–Z; the two-icon validation.
- **Same-name dishes (author's question, same day):** size and price only separate the two «Тыквенный суп» for someone who knows the portions, and the leaf icon can't separate the two «Грибная кружка» (both veg, same subsection). What does, in every pair, is what the guest reads: the **description**. The «Б - Блюда» table gained an «Описание» column (RU) and «Раздел меню» now reads `Soups · вегетарианские`; the Relationship picker appends the first 40 characters of the description. **Carries over to store A:** the hand-built dish dropdown for highlights / previews / pairings will list the same twins and needs the same label.
- **Sidebar labels for the session (author):** «А - Меню Все в одном» (`menu_section`), «Б - Блюда» (`dish`), «Б - Меню Макет» (`menu_list`) — the letters group each variant's screens; the winner goes back to a plain name when the test is decided. **Sidebar order is set by `menu_position` 6 / 7 / 8** (А, Б - Блюда, Б - Меню Макет, between Posts and Media): the first build used 5 / 6 / 6, position 5 is Posts', and WordPress bumps a taken slot past its neighbours — А landed between the two Бs (author's screenshot). Блюда above Макет is a choice (most edits; create, then place), not a constraint.
- **Switch:** `?menu_store=dishes` (`sweet_pepper_menu_store()` in `inc/menu-data.php`) renders the dishes store; without it the page is the repeater store. Test-only — it goes with the losing store, before any page cache.
- **Seeder:** `tools/menu-seed.php soups --dishes` (same parsing; `--force` deletes the section's dish posts and recreates them). Seeded locally: list 24, dishes 25–30.
- **Verified:** Soups markup identical — baseline / repeater / dishes (diff). Through the CLI on the dishes store: price change, Draft hides a dish and keeps its place, reordering the list reorders the page, the repeater store unaffected; all restored and re-diffed. Field groups attach to the right post types; an Editor can edit dishes and cannot add a list. **Not seen: the admin screens themselves** — no login from the CLI; first look is the author's.
- **Removal, if the repeater wins:** `inc/menu-data-dishes.php` + its `require`, the `dish` and `menu_list` registrations, the two JSON groups and their block in the generator, the seeder's `--dishes` branch, the store switch. If the dishes store wins: `menu_section`, its group, `dish_id` filling, and `menu_list` is renamed into its place.

Also confirmed by the author the same day: the Lemon closed call button, the rail's entrance and 2.5 s nudge timing, the Visit page's phone number colour in Safari. `website-brief.md`'s rail paragraph rewritten from a three-attempt running account into the settled cause, the fix and three rules to carry. **What stays from the two attempts that weren't the cause:** whole-pixel rail geometry, and the sticky bar / scrolling row split — both correct in themselves.

## Rail tremble — found: a section-level `overflow-x: clip` — 21 September 2026

The switches did their job. On the author's phones **`?debug=noreveal` and `?debug=noclip` stop the tremble; `norail` and `noscrollanim` don't.** Common to the two: `.has-reveal { overflow-x: clip }`, which `reveal.js → arm()` added to the section of *every* reveal root. The clip is there for `mask-right` only (parked 100% to the right, it would widen the page); `rise`, `mask-up`, `mask-down` and `mask-left` cannot. `arm()` now tags a section only when the root or a nested reveal is `mask-right`. Result per page — home: bar-preview, about-preview · menu: pairing-station, location · drinks: pairing-station-bar, location · about: concept, story, team, location · visit: visit-map; no menu section. Verified at 402 / 768 / 992 / 1280 / 1440 on all five URLs, at load (everything parked) and scrolling through: `scrollWidth === innerWidth`, no `mask-right` element outside a clipped section. `#page`'s clip (≤ 991) stays — `noreveal` left it on and the rail was still; `?debug=nopageclip` added in case. *(Confirmed on the phones and the `?debug=` code deleted the same day — entry above.)*

## Rail tremble, round three — diagnostic switches — 21 September 2026

Splitting the sticky bar from the scrolling row (entry below) did **not** cure the tremble on the phones. The measurement stands — a sticky layer placed a few pixels off, in proportion to scroll speed, while the fixed header is still — so the working theory is now: **something makes WebKit commit a new layer tree from the main thread while the page is scrolling, and each commit places the sticky layer with a stale scroll offset** (a fixed layer has no scroll term, which is why the header never moves). The author suspects the nudge; it fits the mechanism but runs 1.1 s in every 2.5, and the first recording trembles continuously. Other things that work on every scroll frame in the rail's own section: the photo band's `band-drift` (a scroll-driven animation of a *custom property* driving `object-position` — main-thread in any engine; Safari 26 now runs `animation-timeline`, a Mac's Chrome keeps sticky on the compositor regardless), the connector parallax, `reveal.js`'s scroll listener, and two `overflow-x: clip` ancestors (`#page`, `.has-reveal`). **No simulator here, so the phone decides:** `?debug=norail | noscrollanim | noreveal | noclip` (comma-separated, or `all`) — `inc/daypart-head.php` sets `html.debug-*` before paint; `main.css` → *Diagnostics*, `reveal.js`, `menu-rail-nudge.js` honour them. Each verified locally to switch off exactly its own suspect. *(Temporary; deleted 21 Sep once the cause was confirmed.)*

## Menu rail tremble — the real cause; iOS phone-number colour — 21 September 2026

**Rail.** The whole-pixel fix (entry below) did not cure it: still trembling on two iPhones, Safari and Chrome. The author sent a screen recording; with no ffmpeg on this Mac it was served by a small Range-capable Node server, stepped at 1/60 s in headless Chrome and read through a canvas: rows carrying Chili ink → the wordmark 363–421 **on every frame**, the rail's word 540–552, 544 at rest — ±4 CSS px, tracking scroll speed. A sticky layer repositioned a frame late: WebKit handles `sticky` on the scrolling thread unless the element is also a scroll container. `menu-section.css`: `.menu-section-rail` keeps sticky / background / 8px padding and loses all overflow; `.menu-section-rail__nav` (was `display: contents` at every width) becomes `display: flex` below 992 and takes `overflow-x: auto`, `overflow-y: hidden`, the snap, the gap and the 16px lead-in. `menu-single-section.js → alignRail()` and `menu-rail-nudge.js` address the row. Regression: all 16 sections at 360 / 402 / 768 — rail 54, 8 above and below, word at the gutter, no page overflow; stuck top 76; a sideways swipe on the stuck rail snaps SOUPS to 16; reveal flows, entrance, nudge, reduced motion and 1280 unchanged. *(It was not the cause — the tremble persisted; see the two entries above. The split stays, it is the right structure.)*

**Rail timing, tuned on the phone the same day.** `menu-rail-nudge.js`: `EVERY` 5000 → 2500; the entrance no longer waits for 140 ms of scroll rest (momentum scrolling on a phone delayed it until the rail was already stuck) — it fires from a rAF-throttled scroll handler as the rail crosses 66% of the viewport. Simulated continuous scroll at 1500 and 720 px/s: entrance starts with the rail at 63–64% and all 172px of the photo visible, 19 frames before sticking at the higher speed; nudges at ≈ 2.8 / 5.3 / 7.8 s.

**Phone number.** iOS Safari auto-links phone numbers found in plain text, in its own colour: the Visit hero's "+7 (4852) 911-202" went near-black on Peppercorn. `header.php`: `<meta name="format-detection" content="telephone=no, date=no, address=no, email=no">` — every such line already has a real action beside it (tel: link, copy button, map link); `main.css`: `a[x-apple-data-detectors] { color: inherit … }` as a fallback. A Mac never does this, which is why Local looked right.

## Reveal engine — a parked headline could stay parked (menu rail with no current word) — 20 September 2026

Author's report: in Chrome the phone rail showed no active word. Two holes in `reveal.js`, both about the page changing under a still viewport:

1. **A root that loses its box.** At desktop width the menu headline's reveal root is its wrapper, `.section-title`; at ≤ 991 that wrapper is `display: contents`. Load wide, go narrow (DevTools' device toolbar — or a tablet turning from landscape to portrait) and the root is armed but box-less; `check()` skipped box-less roots forever, so the `<h2>` inside stayed at `translate: 0 100%` behind its clip. Now an **armed root without a box is released** (handed back unplayed — the file's own rule: "if a resize shows it later, it is simply there").
2. **A section switch fires no scroll.** Every phone section lands on the same scroll position, and the engine only looks again on scroll / resize / load. `menu-single-section.js → show()` now dispatches **`reveal:check`**, which `reveal.js` listens for.

Reproduced first (headless: load at 1280 → 450 → `#breakfast`: `is-armed`, clip `inset(0 -24px 100%)`), then re-run: fixed, along with rail taps, the hero commit button, hash loads and the away-and-back path. Regression at 1280 on all four pages: reveals still arm at load (45 / 53 / 83 / 15), play on scroll, nothing left parked in view.

## Bar hours settings — regular week — 20 September 2026

The door hours left the code. **Bar Settings** (SCF options page, empty until now) has four time pickers — opens Mon–Sat, opens Sunday, closes Sun–Thu nights, closes Fri/Sat nights (`acf-json/group_sp_bar_hours.json`, RU labels). Defaults 08:30 · 10:00 · 02:00 · 02:00 live in the code, so with nothing saved the site is unchanged. Spec, reasoning and the holiday repeater (specified, not built): `website-brief.md` → Platform → *Bar hours settings*.

- New `inc/bar-hours.php` — `sweet_pepper_bar_hours()` (minutes; a close after midnight is stored past 24:00; per-field sanity check with fallback), `sweet_pepper_bar_hours_rows()` for print. Required in `functions.php` before `daypart-head.php`.
- `inc/daypart-head.php` — prints the hours and defines `window.spBar = { hours, status() }`; `status()` → `{ day, mins, open, opens, closed }` on the bar's clock, and knows a night belongs to the day its doors opened. The daypart thresholds and the 04:00 switch stay here.
- `src/js/bar-clock.js` — now `getBarStatus()`, `getBarHours()`, `formatBarTime()` (8:30 · 10 · 12). `getBarTime()` is gone. `reserve-drawer.js`, `visit-hero.js`, `daypart-engine.js` all ask it; none holds a door time any more (Visit's kitchen stages and the drawer's rush hour still do).
- Times inside copy follow the settings: the three closed subheads and the drawer's two closed lines (`{opens}`). Side effect: the drawer no longer says "from 8:30" on a Sunday morning.
- `footer.php`, `template-parts/visit/hero.php` — hours printed from the helper; a Fri–Sat row appears only if that night ends differently.
- Verified: frozen-clock suite on defaults — identical to the constants at every boundary (Mon 01:30 → Sun 10:00). Then with test values saved locally (opens 09:15, Fri/Sat close 04:30, and a nonsense "closes 14:00"): 09:15 everywhere incl. copy, Sat 03:00 still open on Friday's night, three printed rows, the nonsense value fell back to 02:00. Test values deleted afterwards.

## Home hero closed state — built — 20 September 2026

## One bar clock — 20 September 2026

Found in real-browser testing: the pages and the drawer disagreed about the hour. The home hero (`inc/daypart-head.php`) and the reserve drawer read the visitor's clock; only the Visit hero read Moscow's. **Decided (author): everything follows the bar's clock** — the site shows the bar's state, not the guest's afternoon.

- New `src/js/bar-clock.js` — `getBarTime()` → `{ day, hour, minute }` in `Europe/Moscow` (`BAR_TIME_ZONE`) *(replaced later the same day by `getBarStatus()` — see Bar hours settings)*. `reserve-drawer.js` and `visit-hero.js` import it; `visit-hero.js` lost its private `getMoscowTime()`.
- `inc/daypart-head.php` is inline and can't import: it reads the same zone through `Intl`, falling back to the visitor's clock if the zone isn't supported.
- The drawer's weekday (Sunday 10:00 opening, Fri/Sat busy) is now the bar's weekday too.
- Verified in headless Chrome with the visitor's zone overridden (Los Angeles 10:15, Tokyo 02:15 · Moscow Sun 20:15): home `data-now="dinner"`, night theme, drawer `available`, Visit bar/kitchen `open` in both.

**Visit hero, Sunday (same day):** `getVenueState()` now opens at 10:00 on Sundays (`day === 0`) — bar and kitchen closed for cleaning till 10, the drawer's rule. ~~**Found, not changed:** the hour thresholds in `daypart-head.php` still send 00:00–12:00 to breakfast~~ — fixed the same day with the closed state (next entry). Recorded in (`website-brief.md` → *Open (desktop hero)* → Closed state).
